```
   *  锁定到替代矿(221/222/223)再 ×0.9（:48991 等,替代矿更稀有）；
   *  矿种按世界锁定（SavedOreTiers :48981-49030：每档首砸 roll 一次 genRand.Next(2)==0
   *  → 替代矿,此后全世界固定,随存档持久化 world.savedOreTiers——此前每砸重 roll
   *  会导致同世界混出钴+钯）；深度带按锁定矿种（:49033-49040：107/221=地表线、
   *  108/222=岩层、111/223=(岩层×2+h)/3）；每脉 OreRunner Next(5,10) 恒定（:49045,
   *  旧实现的 5..9+w/4200 是误读）;最后生成 1-2 只幽灵(82)。祭坛 3×2 整体移除。
   *  计数存 world.altarCount（原版 WorldGen.altarCount 静态位，WorldFile.cs:1303/2100
   *  随存档持久化——跨会话保留；海盗入侵自然 roll 的 >0 门同源 Main.cs:64938） */
  private smashAltar(tx: number, ty: number): void {
    const st = this.world.store;
    const ALTAR = TILE_BY_KEY['v_26_altars'];
    // 3×2 对象拆除（TileObjectData 语义）：frameX = style×54 + 列×18 → 列号 %3;
    // frameY/18∈{0,1} 行。锚点 = 左上格——此前扫 ±3 邻域会误拆 3 格内相邻祭坛
    const aIdx = st.idx(tx, ty);
    const ax = tx - (Math.floor(st.frameX[aIdx] / 18) % 3);
    const ay = ty - Math.floor(st.frameY[aIdx] / 18);
    for (let dx = 0; dx < 3; dx++) {
      for (let dy = 0; dy < 2; dy++) {
        if (!st.inBounds(ax + dx, ay + dy)) continue;
        if (st.type[st.idx(ax + dx, ay + dy)] === ALTAR) st.setTileSilent(ax + dx, ay + dy, 0);
      }
    }
    this.chunks.markDirtyArea(ax - 1, ay - 1, ax + 3, ay + 2);
    this.spawnParticles(tx * TILE + 24, ty * TILE + 8, '#B080FF', 14, 2.5);
    this.sfx.play('hit', 1);
    const tier = this.world.altarCount % 3;
    const wave = Math.floor(this.world.altarCount / 3) + 1;
    // 世界锁定选种（SavedOreTiers）：每档 base/alt 二选一,首砸 roll 后固定
    const TIER_ORES: Array<{ slot: 'cobalt' | 'mythril' | 'adamantite'; base: number; alt: number }> = [
      { slot: 'cobalt', base: 107, alt: 221 },
      { slot: 'mythril', base: 108, alt: 222 },
      { slot: 'adamantite', base: 111, alt: 223 },
    ];
    const td = TIER_ORES[tier];
    if (this.world.savedOreTiers[td.slot] === -1) {
      this.world.savedOreTiers[td.slot] = Math.random() < 0.5 ? td.base : td.alt; // Next(2)==0 → 替代
    }
    const oreSheet = this.world.savedOreTiers[td.slot];
    const ore = TILE_INTERNAL_BY_SHEET.get(oreSheet) ?? TILE_BY_KEY['ore_cobalt'];
    // 世界公告（:48991/:49008/:49021——ChatHelper misc[12/13/14 基础,+9=替代矿变体],
    // 按锁定矿种选文案）
    this.newText(Lang.misc((tier === 0 ? 12 : tier === 1 ? 13 : 14) + (oreSheet !== td.base ? 9 : 0)), 175, 75, 255);
    let veins = (st.w / 4200) * 310 - 85 * tier;
    veins *= 0.85;
    veins /= wave;
    if (tier === 0) veins *= 1.05;              // 钴档恒 ×1.05（:49008）
    if (oreSheet !== td.base) veins *= 0.9;     // 替代矿更稀有（:48991/:49016/:49029）
    const band = tier === 0 ? this.world.groundLevel : tier === 1 ? this.world.rockLevel : (this.world.rockLevel * 2 + st.h) / 3;
    const rng = new RNG((Math.random() * 1e9) | 0);
    const cap = Math.floor(veins);
    for (let k = 0; k < cap; k++) {
      const x = 100 + Math.floor(Math.random() * (st.w - 200));
      const y = Math.floor(band) + Math.floor(Math.random() * Math.max(1, st.h - 150 - Math.floor(band)));
      const r = rng.int(5, 9);   // Next(5,10) 恒定区间(:49045)——旧 5..9+w/4200 系误读
      tileRunner(st, rng, lastGenState() ?? { worldSurface: this.world.groundLevel, lavaLine: st.h - 200, waterLine: this.world.groundLevel } as never, x, y, { strength: r, steps: r, type: ore, addTile: true });
    }
    const wraiths = 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 2);
    for (let k = 0; k < wraiths; k++) {
      const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(82, this.player.cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 64, this.player.y - 32);
      if (e) {
        e.id = this.entities.nextId++;
        this.entities.enemies.push(e);
      }
    }
    this.world.altarCount++;
    // 成就：BEGONE_EVIL（WorldGen.cs:49111 event 6——每次砸祭坛都触发）
    this.achievements.notifyProgressionEvent(6);
  }

  private tryMine(tx: number, ty: number, power: number, toolType: 'pick' | 'axe' | 'hammer', speed: number, useTime?: number) {
    // 联机破坏保护（v3 房间制）：非房主禁挖/禁锤墙
    if (!this.netCanEditTile()) { this.mining = null; this.netDeny(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.RoomProtectTiles')); return; }
    // 范围检查
    if (!this.inTileRange(tx, ty)) { this.mining = null; return; }
    const type = this.world.store.get(tx, ty);
```

```
  /** WorldGen.SolidTile：active && tileSolid && !solidTop && !half && slope==0 */
  private solidTile(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    const st = this.host.store;
    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
    const i = st.idx(x, y);
    if (!st.flags[i]) return false;
    const d = TILE_DEFS[st.type[i]];
    return !!d && d.solid && !d.platform && !st.half[i] && st.slope[i] === 0;
  }
  /** PlayerLOS 近似：50 格内有玩家视为可见（原版逐玩家 Collision.CanHitLine 扫描） */
  private playerLOS(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    const p = this.host.playerTile();
    if (!p) return false;
    return Math.abs(p.x - x) < 50 && Math.abs(p.y - y) < 40;
  }
  private mechAny(): boolean {
    const f = this.host.flags;
    return !!(f['downed_125'] || f['downed_126'] || f['downed_127'] || f['downed_134']);
  }
  private mechAll(): boolean {
    const f = this.host.flags;
    // 原版 downedMechBoss1/2/3 = 毁灭者(134)/双子(125)/机械骷髅王(127)
    return !!(f['downed_134'] && f['downed_125'] && f['downed_127']);
  }

  /** 放置 1×1 装饰（帧 style×18,0；目标须为空——PlaceTile EmptyTile 门） */
  private place1(x: number, y: number, sheet: number, style = 0, variants = 1): boolean {
    const st = this.host.store;
    if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || this.active(x, y)) return false;
    const t = S(sheet);
    if (!t) return false;
    st.setTile(x, y, t, (style + (variants > 1 ? Math.floor(Math.random() * variants) : 0)) * 18, 0);
    return true;
  }
  /** 放置 w×h 整件（帧 dx*18, dy*18；区域全空才放）。styleX=样式基偏移（X 轴，
   *  如 2×2 横排样式族 style*36，TileObjectData StyleHorizontal=true） */
  private placeBlock(x: number, y: number, sheet: number, w: number, h: number, styleX = 0): boolean {
    const st = this.host.store;
    for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++) for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) {
      if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, y + dy) || this.active(x + dx, y + dy)) return false;
    }
    const t = S(sheet);
    if (!t) return false;
    for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++) for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) {
      st.setTile(x + dx, y + dy, t, styleX + dx * 18, dy * 18);
    }
    return true;
  }

  // ================= 保底世花球茎（GeneratePlanteraBulbOnAllMechsDefeated） =================

  /** WorldGen.GeneratePlanteraBulbOnAllMechsDefeated（cs:74180-74248）+ AttemptTo
   *  GeneratePlanteraBulbAt（cs:74250-74329）：三王全灭时在丛林侧搜索框
   *  [worldSurface, UnderworldLayer) 内掷 2500 点，逐点向上走找丛林草 60
   *  （<500 次后 forceBulb 允许泥 59，<200 次后允许非活动格）；找到即试
   *  (X,Y-1)/(X-1,Y-1)/(X+1,Y-1) 三锚，**恰好生成一个**灯泡即返回。 */
  planteraBulbBurst(): boolean {
    const st = this.host.store;
    const { w } = st;
    const jungleLeft = this.host.dungeonX > w * 0.5;   // 丛林在地牢对侧（cs:74182）
    const x0 = Math.floor(jungleLeft ? w * 0.15 : w * 0.65);
    const x1 = Math.floor(jungleLeft ? w * 0.35 : w * 0.85);
    const yTop = Math.floor(this.host.worldSurface);
    const yBot = st.h - 200;                            // Main.UnderworldLayer（Main.cs:2863）
    let tries = 2500;
    while (tries > 0) {
      const forceBulb = tries < 500;                     // cs:74197 允许泥 59+计分档
      const allowInactive = tries < 200;                 // cs:74198
      tries--;
      let px = x0 + Math.floor(Math.random() * (x1 - x0));
      let py = yTop + Math.floor(Math.random() * (yBot - yTop));
      // 逐点向上走（≤500 步,cs:74206-74230）：flag2 时非活动格也算；命中 60/59 停
      let foundSheet = -1;
      let steps = 500;
      while (steps-- > 0) {
        py--;
        if (py < yTop) break;
        const sheet = sheetOf(this.typeAt(px, py));
        const act = this.active(px, py);
        if (allowInactive || act) {
          if (sheet === 60 || (forceBulb && sheet === 59)) { foundSheet = sheet; break; }
        }
      }
      if (foundSheet < 0) continue;
      // 三锚尝试（cs:74232-74246）：PlaceJunglePlant 锚=2×2 右下角 → placeBlock 左上角
      for (const ox of [0, -1, 1]) {
        if (this.attemptBulbAt(px + ox, py - 1, forceBulb)) return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }

```

```
	public static void Convert_ActuallyConvertTorch(int conversionType, int x, int y, ref Tile theTile, int newStyle)
	{
		if (!theTile.active() || theTile.type != 4)
		{
			return;
		}
		int num = theTile.frameY / 22;
		if (conversionType == 0 && num == 22)
		{
			newStyle = 21;
		}
		if (num != newStyle)
		{
			bool num2 = conversionType == 1 || conversionType == 4 || conversionType == 2;
			theTile.frameY = (short)(newStyle * 22);
			if (num2 && SpecialSeedFeatures.RainbowSandAndBlackSandWalls)
			{
				paintTile(x, y, 0, broadCast: true);
				paintCoatTile(x, y, 0, broadcast: true);
			}
			SquareTileFrame(x, y);
			NetMessage.SendTileSquare(-1, x, y);
		}
	}

	private static void Convert_ActuallyConvertTile(int conversionType, int x, int y, ref Tile theTile, int newType)
	{
		if (theTile.active() && theTile.type != newType)
		{
			bool num = conversionType == 1 || conversionType == 4 || conversionType == 2;
			TryConvertingOrKillingTreesAboveIfTheyWouldBecomeInvalid(x, y, newType);
			theTile.type = (ushort)newType;
			if (num && SpecialSeedFeatures.RainbowSandAndBlackSandWalls)
			{
				paintTile(x, y, 0, broadCast: true);
				paintCoatTile(x, y, 0, broadcast: true);
			}
			SquareTileFrame(x, y);
			NetMessage.SendTileSquare(-1, x, y);
		}
	}

	private static void Convert_ActuallyConvertWall(int conversionType, int x, int y, ref Tile theTile, int newType)
	{
		if (theTile.wall != 0 && theTile.wall != newType)
		{
			bool num = conversionType == 1 || conversionType == 4 || conversionType == 2;
			theTile.wall = (ushort)newType;
			if (num && SpecialSeedFeatures.RainbowSandAndBlackSandWalls)
			{
				paintWall(x, y, 0, broadCast: true);
				paintCoatWall(x, y, 0, broadcast: true);
			}
			SquareWallFrame(x, y);
			NetMessage.SendTileSquare(-1, x, y);
		}
	}

	public static void CactusFrame(int i, int j)
	{
		try
		{
			int num = j;
			int num2 = i;
			if (CheckCactus(i, j))
			{
				return;
			}
			while (Main.tile[num2, num].active() && Main.tile[num2, num].type == 80)
			{
				num++;
				if (Main.tile[num2, num] == null)
				{
					return;
				}
```

```
30	export function toggleTorchFrame(frameX: number): number {
31	  return isUnlitTorch(frameX) ? frameX - TORCH_UNLIT_OFFSET : frameX + TORCH_UNLIT_OFFSET;

54	  if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) return -1;
55	  if (st.isSolid(tx, ty + 1)) return 0;                       // base: AnchorBottom

68	export function torchStillAnchored(st: TileStore, tx: number, ty: number, frameX: number): boolean {
69	  if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) return false;
```

```
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using Microsoft.Xna.Framework;

namespace Terraria.ID;

public static class TorchID
{
	public class Sets
	{
		public static SetFactory Factory = new SetFactory(Count);

		public static bool[] IsABiomeTorch = Factory.CreateBoolSet(false, 0, 18, 19, 20, 21, 23, 13, 7, 9, 22, 16);
	}

	private interface ITorchLightProvider
	{
		void GetRGB(out float r, out float g, out float b);
	}

	private struct ConstantTorchLight(float Red, float Green, float Blue) : ITorchLightProvider
	{
		public float R = Red;

		public float G = Green;

		public float B = Blue;

		public void GetRGB(out float r, out float g, out float b)
		{
			r = R;
			g = G;
			b = B;
		}
	}

	[StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Size = 1)]
	private struct DemonTorchLight : ITorchLightProvider
	{
		public void GetRGB(out float r, out float g, out float b)
		{
			r = 0.5f * Main.demonTorch + (1f - Main.demonTorch);
			g = 0.3f;
			b = Main.demonTorch + 0.5f * (1f - Main.demonTorch);
		}
	}

	[StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Size = 1)]
	private struct ShimmerTorchLight : ITorchLightProvider
	{
		public void GetRGB(out float r, out float g, out float b)
		{
			float num = 0.9f;
			float num2 = 0.9f;
			num += (float)(270 - Main.mouseTextColor) / 900f;
			num2 += (float)(270 - Main.mouseTextColor) / 125f;
			num = MathHelper.Clamp(num, 0f, 1f);
			num2 = MathHelper.Clamp(num2, 0f, 1f);
			r = num * 0.9f;
			g = num2 * 0.55f;
			b = num * 1.2f;

```

```
	public static bool TileIsExposedToAir(int x, int y)
	{
		if (!InWorld(x, y, 2))
		{
			return false;
		}
		for (int i = x - 1; i <= x + 1; i++)
		{
			for (int j = y - 1; j <= y + 1; j++)
			{
				Tile tile = Main.tile[i, j];
				if (tile != null && (!tile.active() || !Main.tileSolid[tile.type] || TileID.Sets.Platforms[tile.type]))
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static bool TryConvertingOrKillingTreesAboveIfTheyWouldBecomeInvalid(int i, int j, int newFloorType)
```

```
    return x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < this.w && y < this.h;
  }

  get(x: number, y: number): number { return this.inBounds(x, y) ? this.type[this.idx(x, y)] : 0; }

  isActive(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    // 原版 nactive() = active && !inActive（Tile.cs:383-394）：已致动格视为不活跃
    return this.inBounds(x, y) && this.type[this.idx(x, y)] !== 0
      && (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) === 0;
  }

  /** 原版 inActive()：致动器翻转后的"缩入地下"态（有 type 但不碰撞不阻挡） */
  isActuated(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    return this.inBounds(x, y) && (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) !== 0;
  }

  isSolid(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return false; // 世界外视为空（边缘自由）
    if (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) return false; // 已致动不碰撞
    const d = TILE_DEFS[this.type[this.idx(x, y)]];
    return !!d && d.solid;
  }

  isPlatform(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
    const d = TILE_DEFS[this.type[this.idx(x, y)]];
    return !!d && d.platform;
  }

  onTileChanged(fn: (x: number, y: number) => void) {
    this.listeners.push(fn);
  }

  /** 液体变化独立通道：chunk 缓存不订阅（水不入烘焙画布，无需重建），
   *  只有光照订阅（水的光衰减）——避免水流时每帧冲爆 chunk 重建队列造成卡顿 */
  private liquidListeners: Array<(x: number, y: number) => void> = [];
  onLiquidChanged(fn: (x: number, y: number) => void) {
    this.liquidListeners.push(fn);
  }

  /** 唯一的写入入口（生成/导入期可绕过用 setTileSilent） */
  setTile(x: number, y: number, type: number, frameX = 0, frameY = 0) {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
    const i = this.idx(x, y);
    const wasActive = this.flags[i] !== 0;
    this.type[i] = type;
    this.flags[i] = type !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
    this.frameX[i] = frameX;
    this.frameY[i] = frameY;
    if (type === 0) { this.half[i] = 0; this.slope[i] = 0; } // 挖除清半砖/坡面
    // 油漆随方块清除/铺块重置：
    //   KillTile（WorldGen.cs:63933-63939）active(false)+halfBrick(false)+ClearBlockPaintAndCoating；
    //   PlaceTile（WorldGen.cs:59499-59501）!active() 时 Clear(Tile|TilePaint|Slope)。
    //   即油漆只在 active→active 的替换（草化/转化）中存活
    if (type === 0 || !wasActive) this.paint[i] = 0;
    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetTile, x, y, v: type, fx: frameX, fy: frameY });
    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
  }

  /** 运行期改半砖/坡面位（锤子交互用；生成期直接写数组即可） */
  setHalfBrick(x: number, y: number, v: boolean) {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
    const i = this.idx(x, y);
    this.half[i] = v ? 1 : 0;
    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetHalfBrick, x, y, v: v ? 1 : 0, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
  }

  setSlope(x: number, y: number, s: number) {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
    const i = this.idx(x, y);
    this.slope[i] = s;
    this.half[i] = 0; // 原版 SlopeTile 无条件 halfBrick(false)（WorldGen.cs:49174），slope=0 复位时同样
    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetSlope, x, y, v: s, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
  }

  setTileSilent(x: number, y: number, type: number, frameX = 0, frameY = 0) {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
    const i = this.idx(x, y);
    const wasActive = this.flags[i] !== 0;
    this.type[i] = type;
    this.flags[i] = type !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
    this.frameX[i] = frameX;
    this.frameY[i] = frameY;
    if (type === 0) { this.half[i] = 0; this.slope[i] = 0; } // 挖除同步清位（防生成期脏残留）
    if (type === 0 || !wasActive) this.paint[i] = 0;          // KillTile/PlaceTile 油漆清位（同 setTile）
  }

  setWall(x: number, y: number, wall: number) {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
    const i = this.idx(x, y);
    const hadWall = this.wall[i] !== 0;
    if (this.wall[i] !== wall) {
      this.wall[i] = wall;
      // 墙漆清位：KillWall（WorldGen.cs:60425-60426）wall=0 后 ClearWallPaintAndCoating；
      // PlaceWall（:75199-75201）本身不清 wallColor——但 KillWall 已保证 wall==0 ⇒ 无漆，
      // 故 0→非0 铺墙时同样归零（保持原版不可达态不变量，见 world/Paint.ts）
      if (wall === 0 || !hadWall) this.paintWall[i] = 0;
      if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetWall, x, y, v: wall, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
      this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
    }
  }

  setWallSilent(x: number, y: number, wall: number) {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
    const i = this.idx(x, y);
    const hadWall = this.wall[i] !== 0;
    this.wall[i] = wall;
    if (wall === 0 || !hadWall) this.paintWall[i] = 0; // 同 setWall 的墙漆清位
  }

  /** 油漆写入（WorldGen.paintTile/paintWall 等价原语）。发 listeners：油漆改变 tile/wall
   *  视觉色 → chunk 画布与小地图都须重建（导线不进烘焙画布故 setWire 不发，此处不同）。
   *  值域 0-31（PaintID），>31 由调用方保证不出现（Item.paint 为 byte） */
  setPaint(x: number, y: number, v: number) {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
    const i = this.idx(x, y);
    if (this.paint[i] === v) return;
    this.paint[i] = v & 31;
    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
  }

  setPaintSilent(x: number, y: number, v: number) {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
    this.paint[this.idx(x, y)] = v & 31;
  }

  /** 墙漆写入（WorldGen.paintWall） */
  setPaintWall(x: number, y: number, v: number) {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
    const i = this.idx(x, y);
    if (this.paintWall[i] === v) return;
    this.paintWall[i] = v & 31;
    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
  }

  setPaintWallSilent(x: number, y: number, v: number) {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
    this.paintWall[this.idx(x, y)] = v & 31;
  }

  /** 导线/致动位写入（Wiring 系统）。不发 listeners：导线不进 chunk 烘焙画布，
   *  走 Renderer 动态覆盖层；致动位变化才需要标脏 chunk（调用方负责） */
  setWire(x: number, y: number, bits: number) {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
    this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] = bits & 63;
    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetWire, x, y, v: bits & 63, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
  }

  setWireSilent(x: number, y: number, bits: number) {
    this.setWire(x, y, bits);
  }

  /** 致动器翻转 inActive 态（Wiring.DeActive/ReActive 用）：影响碰撞与渲染，须发 listeners */
  setActuated(x: number, y: number, v: boolean) {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
    const i = this.idx(x, y);
    const b = v ? (this.wire[i] | TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) : (this.wire[i] & ~TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED);
    if (this.wire[i] === b) return;
    this.wire[i] = b;
    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetActuated, x, y, v: v ? 1 : 0, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
  }

  setLiquid(x: number, y: number, amount: number, type: number) {
```

```
// ⑤ 空水格睡莲/香蒲（WorldGen.cs:72237-72248，各 1/600）

describe('WorldEvolution 空水格睡莲/香蒲', () => {
  function evoWorld() {
    const w = new World(60, 60, 7, 'evo');
    const st = w.store;
    const y = 30;
    st.setTileSilent(30, y + 2, 1);            // 水底
    st.liquid[st.idx(30, y)] = 255;            // 满水空格
    const host: EvolutionHost = {
      store: st, worldSurface: 50, rockLevel: 60,
      flags: w.flags, crimson: false, isDay: true, raining: false,
      maxRaining: 0, windSpeedCurrent: 0, playerTile: () => null,
      dungeonX: 0,   // Main.dungeonX（保底灯泡搜索框；本测试无地牢）
    };
    return { w, st, y, evo: new WorldEvolution(host) };
  }
  type EvoLike = { overgroundTile: (i: number, j: number, wallDist: number) => void };

  it('空水格 1/600 → 睡莲 518（cs:72237-72242）', () => {
    const { st, y, evo } = evoWorld();
    const spy = vi.spyOn(Math, 'random').mockReturnValue(0);   // 必中小概率门
    try {
      (evo as unknown as EvoLike).overgroundTile(30, y, 3);
    } finally {
      spy.mockRestore();
    }
    expect(st.type[st.idx(30, y)]).not.toBe(0);
  });

  it('首掷未中 → 香蒲 519（else-if 第二档 :72243-72248）', () => {
    const { st, y, evo } = evoWorld();
    let calls = 0;
    const spy = vi.spyOn(Math, 'random').mockImplementation(() => (calls++ === 0 ? 0.99 : 0));
    try {
      (evo as unknown as EvoLike).overgroundTile(30, y, 3);
    } finally {
      spy.mockRestore();
    }
    expect(st.type[st.idx(30, y)]).not.toBe(0);
  });
});
```

```
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { MUSIC, BOSS_MUSIC, bossMusicFor, pickMusic, newMusicState } from '../src/data/Music';
import { EMPTY_SCENE } from '../src/world/SceneMetrics';

describe('Boss 专属 BGM（Main.cs:12162-12280 + 12474-12535）', () => {
  it('映射表逐项对照原版', () => {
    expect(bossMusicFor(4)).toBe(MUSIC.Boss1);            // EoC → 默认 Boss1
    expect(bossMusicFor(13)).toBe(99);                    // EoW
    expect(bossMusicFor(35)).toBe(104);                   // 骷髅王
    expect(bossMusicFor(50)).toBe(93);                    // 史莱姆王
    expect(bossMusicFor(113)).toBe(12);                   // 血肉墙 Boss2
    expect(bossMusicFor(125)).toBe(97);                   // 双子
    expect(bossMusicFor(127)).toBe(98);                   // Prime
    expect(bossMusicFor(134)).toBe(92);                   // 毁灭者
    expect(bossMusicFor(222)).toBe(96);                   // 蜂后
    expect(bossMusicFor(245)).toBe(17);                   // 石巨人
    expect(bossMusicFor(262)).toBe(24);                   // 世花
    expect(bossMusicFor(266)).toBe(13);                   // 克脑 Boss3
    expect(bossMusicFor(370)).toBe(58);                   // 猪鲨
    expect(bossMusicFor(398)).toBe(38);                   // 月总
    expect(bossMusicFor(439)).toBe(94);                   // 异教徒
    expect(bossMusicFor(636)).toBe(57);                   // 光皇
    expect(bossMusicFor(657)).toBe(56);                   // 史莱姆皇后
    expect(bossMusicFor(668)).toBe(90);                   // 鹿角怪
    expect(bossMusicFor(999)).toBe(MUSIC.Boss1);          // 未列出 boss → Boss1
    // 段 id 全覆盖（13/14/15 同曲）
    expect(BOSS_MUSIC[14]).toBe(BOSS_MUSIC[13]);
    expect(BOSS_MUSIC[15]).toBe(BOSS_MUSIC[13]);
  });

  it('pickMusic：Boss 曲先于一切群系分支（含地狱/城镇）', () => {
    const scene = { ...EMPTY_SCENE, townNPCCount: 0 } as never;
    const base = {
      scene, isDay: true,
      worldSurface: 300, rockLevel: 500, lavaLine: 1000,
      maxTilesX: 4200, centerX: 200 * 16, centerY: 1100 * 16, // 地狱深度
      state: newMusicState(),
    };
    const withBoss = pickMusic({ ...base, bossMusic: 96 } as never);
    expect(withBoss).toBe(96);                              // 蜂后曲压过地狱
    const without = pickMusic({ ...base, bossMusic: 0 } as never);
    expect(without).toBe(MUSIC.Underworld);                 // 无 boss 落地狱曲
  });
});
```

```
// 肉山死亡链无头实证（NPC.cs:80281-80292 + WorldGen.cs:31866/79837）：
//   砖盒（边缘非活动格→魔金砖140/猩红347、全盒清液）+ StartHardmode
//   （hardMode 置位 + V 带神圣/邪恶转化 + 洞穴墙回填）。
//   ★新三矿与"赐福"公告不在本链——原版由砸祭坛触发（SmashAltar misc[12-14]）。
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { TILE_DEFS, TILE_BY_KEY } from '../src/data/tiles';
import { createBrickBoxForWallOfFlesh, startHardmode } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/HardmodePass';

const W = 900, H = 400;
const STONE = TILE_BY_KEY['stone']!;   // 内部 id 1 是 dirt——V 带只转石/草/沙,勿用裸土

function makeWorld(): World {
  const w = new World(W, H, 7, 'wof-chain-test');
  const st = w.store;
  w.groundLevel = 120;
  w.rockLevel = 200;
  // 表层草 + 全实心石世界（V 带转化有料可转）
  for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) {
    st.setTile(x, 100, TILE_BY_KEY['grass']!);
    for (let y = 101; y < H; y++) st.setTile(x, y, STONE);
  }
  return w;
}
const sheetOf = (id: number) => TILE_DEFS[id]?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1;

describe('肉山死亡链', () => {
  it('砖盒：边缘非活动格变魔金砖(140)、盒内液体清零', () => {
    const w = makeWorld();
    const st = w.store;
    // 死亡点挖空 13×13 并灌岩浆（模拟地狱环境）
    const cx = 450, cy = 350, r = 6;
    for (let i = cx - r; i <= cx + r; i++) for (let j = cy - r; j <= cy + r; j++) {
      st.setTile(i, j, 0);
      st.liquid[st.idx(i, j)] = 255; st.liquidType[st.idx(i, j)] = 2;
    }
    createBrickBoxForWallOfFlesh(st, cx, cy, 160, false);   // WoF width=160 → r=6
    let shellBrick = 0, innerSolid = 0, liquidLeft = 0;
    for (let i = cx - r; i <= cx + r; i++) for (let j = cy - r; j <= cy + r; j++) {
      const ii = st.idx(i, j);
      if (st.liquid[ii] > 0) liquidLeft++;
      const onShell = i === cx - r || i === cx + r || j === cy - r || j === cy + r;
      if (onShell) {
        if (st.flags[ii] && sheetOf(st.type[ii]) === 140) shellBrick++;
      } else if (st.flags[ii]) innerSolid++;
    }
    expect(shellBrick).toBe(4 * (2 * r + 1) - 4);   // 完整环（全空环境 48 格全填）
    expect(innerSolid).toBe(0);                     // 内部保持空
    expect(liquidLeft).toBe(0);                     // 盒内液体清零
  });

  it('StartHardmode：hardMode 置位 + V 带转化出神圣/邪恶方块', () => {
    const w = makeWorld();
    const before = !!w.flags.hardMode;
    const { wasHard } = startHardmode(w);
    expect(wasHard).toBe(before);
    expect(w.flags.hardMode).toBe(true);
    // V 带扫过实心世界 → 一定出现神圣族(珍珠石117/神圣草109)与邪恶族(黑檀石25/腐化草23)
    const st = w.store;
    let hallow = 0, evil = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < st.type.length; i++) {
      if (!st.flags[i]) continue;
      const sh = sheetOf(st.type[i]);
      if (sh === 117 || sh === 109 || sh === 116) hallow++;
      if (sh === 25 || sh === 23 || sh === 112) evil++;
    }
    expect(hallow).toBeGreaterThan(50);
    expect(evil).toBeGreaterThan(50);
  });
});

```

```
/**
 * 一次性诊断脚本的统一 runner(防孤儿进程)。
 *
 * 背景:2026-08-10 发现 7 个 `vite-node tools/_diag-gen.mjs` 孤儿进程,
 * 每个占满 1 核空转 4 小时(脚本 import 链带起不可退出的活句柄,TERM 信号都无法处理)。
 * 约定:以后所有 _diag-*.mjs 一律经本 runner 运行,不再直接 npm exec vite-node。
 *
 * 用法:
 *   node tools/run-diag.mjs [--timeout=ms] <script.mjs> [传给脚本的参数...]
 *
 * 双层架构:
 *   - 子进程(--_child,内部):导入目标脚本;导入完成 0.5s 后强制 exit(0),
 *     不依赖事件循环自然排空——兜住"脚本主体完成但残留 interval/server 等活句柄"(本次事故形态);
 *   - 父进程(默认):只等子进程退出 + 看门狗;子进程超时(默认 120s)未退 → SIGKILL,exit 2。
 *     父进程事件循环始终空闲,因此子进程无论是同步死循环、永不 resolve 的 await
 *     还是任何未知卡死,都必然在 timeout 内被强杀。
 *
 * 退出码:0 正常 / 1 脚本异常或被信号终止 / 2 超时强杀 / 64 用法错误,其余透传脚本自设退出码。
 */
const DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 120_000;
const FLUSH_MS = 500;

    process.exit(1);

  // 主体完成:给 stdout 冲刷留 0.5s,无视残留活句柄强制正常退出。
  setTimeout(() => process.exit(0), FLUSH_MS).unref?.();
} else {
  // ---------- 父进程模式:spawn 子进程 + 看门狗 ----------

    if (a.startsWith('--timeout=')) {
      const v = Number(a.slice('--timeout='.length));
      if (Number.isFinite(v) && v > 0) timeoutMs = v;
    } else {
      rest.push(a);
    }
  }
  if (!rest.length) {
    console.error('usage: node tools/run-diag.mjs [--timeout=ms] <script.mjs> [args...]');
    process.exit(64);
  }

  const { spawn } = await import('node:child_process');
  const path = await import('node:path');
  const target = path.resolve(process.cwd(), rest[0]);

  const { fileURLToPath } = await import('node:url');
```

```
//   2. 小动物腐化（血月中兔 → 腐化兔 47 敌怪）
//   3. 血泪 4271：夜用 → bloodMoon=true + 新月拨 5 + misc[8] 公告；血月中再用 → 无效

import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome', headless: true, args: ['--no-sandbox'], defaultViewport: { width: 1360, height: 860 } });
const page = await browser.newPage();
const errs = [];
page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push('pageerror: ' + e.message));
await page.goto('' + (process.env.SW_ORIGIN ?? 'http://localhost:5199') + '', { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });
await page.waitForSelector('.sw-title-menu', { timeout: 120000 });
await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 400));
await page.click('[data-act=sp]');
await page.waitForSelector('.sw-list-panel', { timeout: 10000 });
await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 800));
const hasChar = await page.evaluate(() => !!document.querySelector('.sw-item'));
if (!hasChar) {
  await page.click('.sw-list-footer [data-act=new]');
  await page.waitForSelector('.sw-char-panel', { timeout: 10000 });
  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 500));
  await page.click('.sw-char-row input[type=text]');
  await page.keyboard.type('血月测试');
  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 200));
  await page.click('[data-act=create]');
  await page.waitForSelector('.sw-item', { timeout: 10000 });
  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 600));
}
await page.click('.sw-item');
await page.waitForFunction(() => document.querySelector('.sw-list-title')?.textContent === '选择世界', { timeout: 10000 });
await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 700));
const hasWorld = await page.evaluate(() => !!document.querySelector('.sw-list-panel .sw-item'));
if (!hasWorld) {
  await page.click('.sw-list-footer [data-act=new]');
  await page.waitForSelector('.sw-wc-panel', { timeout: 10000 });
  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 500));
  await page.click('[data-g=size] .sw-wc-opt');
  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 200));
  await page.click('[data-act=create]');
}
await page.waitForFunction(() => { const g = window.__swGame; return g && g.player; }, { timeout: 180000 });
await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 2000));

const out = await page.evaluate(async () => {
  const g = window.__swGame;
  const w = g.world;
  const res = {};
  // 入夜 + 放一只兔（小动物）在玩家旁
  w.clock.timeOfDay = 0.9; // 夜（19:30 后）
  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 300));
```

````
# SandboxWorld 项目约定

泰拉瑞亚 1.4.5.6 复刻(TypeScript + Canvas + Vite,主体代码在 `game/`)。
反编译源码是唯一标杆:`Terarria1456/`(1.4.5.6 全量,数值与行为以此为准)、
`Terarria1405/` 仅作交叉参照;报异常先查反编译源码/TEdit 校对再修,禁止凭感觉改。

## 并行会话 vite 防打断约定(必须遵守)

本机常有多条 Claude Code 并行会话共用这份代码。**所有会话共用 5199 一个 dev
server,任一会话改源码会触发 HMR 全页重载,把其他会话正在跑的 puppeteer
探针页面撕掉。** 凡是需要跑浏览器探针(scripts/_*.mjs 经 tools/run-diag.mjs)的会话:

1. **起自己的"私有静默实例"**(端口按会话错开 5201/5202/5203…,先 pgrep
   确认没被占):
   ```sh
   SW_PORT=5201 SW_NO_HMR=1 SW_CACHE=/tmp/sw-vite-5201 \
     nohup npx vite > /tmp/vite-5201.log 2>&1 &
   ```
2. **探针一律带自己的 origin**:
   ```sh
   SW_ORIGIN=http://localhost:5201 node tools/run-diag.mjs scripts/_xxx.mjs
   ```
   (全部探针脚本已支持 SW_ORIGIN,缺省回退 5199)
3. **禁止 kill 5199**(共享实例,人工试玩用);也禁止动别人的 52xx 端口。
4. 会话收尾 kill 自己的 52xx 实例(先 pgrep 再 kill,防孤儿进程)。
5. 纯 vitest 单测不需要 server,照常跑。

说明:`SW_NO_HMR=1` 只关闭向已连接页面的 reload 推送,watcher 与代码新鲜度
不受影响——探针每次 page.goto 仍加载最新源码。**编辑 vite.config.ts 会让所有
运行中的 vite 实例各重启一次,非必要不动它。**

## 其他硬约定

- 一次性诊断脚本(`_` 前缀)一律经 `node tools/run-diag.mjs` 运行(在 `game/`
  目录下执行),禁止裸 vite-node;删文件前先 pgrep 确认无关联进程。
- 1456 版数值/行为一律取最终态;1405 与 1456 冲突时以 1456 为准并在
  注释标明两版行号。
- l10n-audit 会阻断 vitest/build:新增 `Mods.SandboxWorld.*` 键后先在
  `tools/l10n-custom/{zh-Hans,en-US}.json` 补键,再在 `game/` 下
  `node scripts/build-l10n.mjs` 重建 `public/l10n/` 产物。

````

```
82	  for (let i = 0; i < 90; i++) {
83	    g.fixedUpdate(1 / 60);
84	    if (g.entities.projectiles.some((p) => p.dead)) break;
85	  }
86	  g.input.mouseDown = false;
87	  const potGone = st.get(px0 + 4, py - 2) !== POT && st.get(px0 + 5, py - 3) !== POT;
88	  const grassGone = st.get(px0 + 10, py) !== TALL;
89	  const arrowsAfter = g.player.inv.slots.reduce((s, it) => s + (it && it.id === arrowId ? it.stack : 0), 0);
90	  return {
91	    projSpeed0, arrowsBefore, arrowsAfter, gravOk, vySeries,
92	    potGone, grassGone,
93	    dropsSeen: g.entities.drops.length,
94	  };
95	});
96	console.log(JSON.stringify(out, null, 1));
97	check('射击生成弹幕（PickAmmo 合并速度 6.1+3=9.1±）', out.projSpeed0.length > 0 && Math.abs(out.projSpeed0[0].speed - 9.1) < 0.5, JSON.stringify(out.projSpeed0));
98	check('弹幕伤害 = 弓 4 + 箭 5 = 9', out.projSpeed0[0]?.damage === 9, `dmg=${out.projSpeed0[0]?.damage}`);
99	check('消耗弹药（每次射击扣 1，含连射）', out.arrowsAfter < out.arrowsBefore, `${out.arrowsBefore}→${out.arrowsAfter}`);
100	check('箭受重力（vy 每tick +0.3）', out.gravOk, JSON.stringify(out.vySeries));
101	check('箭射碎瓦罐（CutTiles 语义）', out.potGone);
102	check('箭砍除草（CutTiles 语义）', out.grassGone);
103	console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
104	if (errors.length) console.log('页面异常:', errors.slice(0, 3));
105	await browser.close();
106	process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
107
```

```
export function viIdFromKey(key: string): number {
  const m = key.match(/^vi_(\d+)_/);
  return m ? Number(m[1]) : -1;
}
```

```
// 原版物品 id → 注册 key/内部 id 的统一查表入口(本地物品退役后全库唯一物品字面量源)。
// ★ 严禁手拼 vi_ 键字符串:蛇形/驼峰双注册下唯一权威是 VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID(驼峰)。
// 后续 dual-key 清理(蛇形/驼峰归一)只需改本文件,消费点无感。
import { VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID, ITEM_BY_KEY } from './items';

/** 原版物品 id → 注册 key(查不到返回 '') */
export function VI(vid: number): string {
  return VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vid] ?? '';
}

/** 原版物品 id → 内部 id(查不到返回 0) */
export function VI_ID(vid: number): number {
  const k = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vid];
  return k !== undefined ? (ITEM_BY_KEY[k] ?? 0) : 0;
}

/** 高频消费点语义常量(grep 唯一入口;数值 = 原版 item id 权威) */
export const VI_KEY = {
  // 基础方块/材料
  DIRT: VI(2), STONE: VI(3), WOOD: VI(9), SAND: VI(169), SNOW: VI(593),
  CLAY: VI(133), GLASS: VI(170), MUD: VI(176), OBSIDIAN: VI(173), SILT: VI(424),
  ICE: VI(664), GRAY_BRICK: VI(129), RED_BRICK: VI(131),
  GEL: VI(23), ACORN: VI(27), MUSHROOM: VI(5), LENS: VI(38), ROPE: VI(965),
  BOMB: VI(166), WIRE: VI(530),
  // 放置/功能物
  TORCH: VI(8), PLATFORM: VI(94), DOOR: VI(25), WORKBENCH: VI(36), FURNACE: VI(33),
  ANVIL: VI(35), CHEST: VI(48), SIGN: VI(171),
  TABLE: VI(32), CHAIR: VI(34), BED: VI(224), TOMBSTONE: VI(321), LIFE_CRYSTAL: VI(29),
```

````
/** TileID.Sets.Conversion.Torch（TileID.cs:42-74）：该 conversionType 下火把可否被转。
 *  case 0 净化：仅邪恶/神圣火把(18-20)+蘑菇(22)；case 11 净化粉：18-20；
 *  其余 case：常规(0)与群系(9 冰/16 沙漠/18-20 邪恶神圣/21 丛林/22 蘑菇)全收 */
function torchConvertible(convType: number, style: number): boolean {
  if (convType === 0) return (style >= 18 && style <= 20) || style === 22;
  if (convType === 11) return style >= 18 && style <= 20;
  return style === 0 || style === 9 || style === 16
    || (style >= 18 && style <= 22);
}
```

/** BlockBelowMakesSandConvertIntoHardenedSand（cs:87307-87327）：下方非活动(nactive)
 *  或非实心 → 沙转硬化沙（露天沙会塌,硬化防塌） */
private blockBelowHardensSand(i: number, j: number): boolean {
  const st = this.host.store;
  if (j >= st.h - 1) return false;
  const k = st.idx(i, j + 1);
  if (!st.flags[k]) return true;                 // 下方空/非活动
  const d = TILE_DEFS[st.type[k]];
  return !!d && !d.solid;                        // 非实心（平台/装饰）
}

/** TileIsExposedToAir（cs:54810-54830）：3×3 内任一格非活动/非实心/平台 → 露天 */
private tileExposedToAir(i: number, j: number): boolean {
  for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
    const x = i + dx, y = j + dy;
    const st = this.host.store;
    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
    const k = st.idx(x, y);
    const d = TILE_DEFS[st.type[k]];
    if (!st.flags[k] || !d?.solid || d.platform) return true;
  }
  return false;
}
```
````

```json
{
```

```json
 "file_path": "~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/world/evolution/WorldEvolution.ts",
 "old_string": "  // ================= Convert（cs:55113，演化路径用 case 1/2/3/4/8/9） =================",
 "new_string": "  // ================= Convert（cs:55113）=================\n  //  演化路径用 case 1/2/3/4/8/9/11；case 5/6/7/10/12/13 为环境转换器/还原链补全\n  //  （当前演化路径未消费,备 Clentaminator/神庙致动还原等将来接入）。\n  //  未移植：case 0（纯净化墙链,蘑菇墙 80→15/64 深度带）与 case 14-21（1.4.4+\n  //  丛林/沙/雪/蘑菇反转换）——无消费方,登记\n\n  /** BlockBelowMakesSandConvertIntoHardenedSand（cs:87307-87327）：下方非 nactive\n   *  或非实心 → 沙转硬化沙（防塌；nactive=active&&!inActive,已致动格视为不支撑） */\n  private blockBelowHardensSand(i: number, j: number): boolean {\n    const st = this.host.store;\n    if (j >= st.h - 1) return false;\n    if (!st.inBounds(i, j + 1)) return false;\n    if (!st.isActive(i, j + 1)) return true;\n    const d = TILE_DEFS[st.type[st.idx(i, j + 1)]];\n    return !!d && !d.solid;\n  }\n\n  /** TileIsExposedToAir（cs:54810-54830）：3×3 内任一格非活动/非实心/平台 → 露天 */\n  private tileExposedToAir(i: number, j: number): boolean {\n    const st = this.host.store;\n    for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {\n      const x = i + dx, y = j + dy;\n      if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;\n      const k = st.idx(x, y);\n      const d = TILE_DEFS[st.type[k]];\n      if (!st.flags[k] || !d?.solid || d.platform) return true;\n    }\n    return false;\n  }\n\n  /** Main.tileFrameImportant[type]——多帧物（家具/火把/门等）不可被\"满转\"吞并 */\n  private frameImportant(sheet: number): boolean {\n    return !!TILE_DEFS[S(sheet)]?.framed;\n  }"
}
```

```
  }

  /** C# NextDouble()（:113-115）*/
  nextDouble(): number {
    return this.sample();
  }

  /** C# Peek()（:88-99，非消耗）：下一 InternalSample 原始差值——对账/断点用 */
  peek(): number {
    let num = this.inext + 1;
    if (num > 55) num = 1;
    let num2 = num + 21;
    if (num2 > 55) num2 -= 55;
    return this.seedArray[num] - this.seedArray[num2];
  }
}

// ── RNG：世界生成/运行时统一入口（API 不变，内核换 UnifiedRandom）──────────
// 与原版调用点的对账约定（gen 代码全部按此书写）：
//   rng.next()   = NextDouble()          每次消耗 1 个 InternalSample
//   rng.int(a,b) = Next(a, b+1)          闭区间 [a,b]
//   rng.chance(p)= NextDouble() < p      1 次
//   rng.pick(xs) = xs[Next(xs.length)]   1 次
//   rng.range(a,b) = a + NextDouble()*(b-a)
export class RNG {
  private ur: UnifiedRandom;
  constructor(seed: number) {
    this.ur = new UnifiedRandom(seed);
  }

  next(): number {
    return this.ur.nextDouble();
  }

  /** 闭区间 [min, max]，≡ 原版 Next(min, max+1) */
  int(min: number, max: number): number {
    return this.ur.nextIntRange(min, max + 1);
  }

  range(min: number, max: number): number {
    return min + this.ur.nextDouble() * (max - min);
  }

  chance(p: number): boolean {
    return this.ur.nextDouble() < p;
  }
```

```
}

function evoAt(jAbove: boolean) {
  const w = new World(24, 24, 7, 'evo');
  const host: EvolutionHost = {
    store: w.store, worldSurface: 12, rockLevel: 18,
    flags: w.flags, crimson: false, isDay: true, raining: false,
    maxRaining: 0, windSpeedCurrent: 0, playerTile: () => null,
    dungeonX: 0,   // Main.dungeonX（保底灯泡搜索框；本测试无地牢）
  };
  return { w, st: w.store, evo: new WorldEvolution(host), j: jAbove ? 6 : 20 };
}

describe('Convert case 11 净化（WorldGen.cs:55792-55902）', () => {
  it.each([
    [25, 1],    // 黑檀石 → 石
    [23, 2],    // 腐化草 → 草
    [112, 53],  // 黑檀沙 → 沙
    [117, 1],   // 珍珠石（神圣）也还原为石——净化对邪恶/神圣双向生效

    [400, 396], // 腐化沙岩 → 沙岩
    [661, 60],  // 腐化丛林草 → 丛林草
  ])('tile sheet %i → %i', (from, to) => {
    const { st, evo, j } = evoAt(false);
    st.setTileSilent(4, j, S(from));
    evo.convert(4, j, 11);
    expect(TILE_DEFS[st.type[st.idx(4, j)]]?.vanilla?.sheet).toBe(to);
  });

  it('墙：腐化草墙 69（地表）→ 原版草墙 63/65 档；石族墙 → 349', () => {
    const above = evoAt(true);
    above.st.setWallSilent(4, above.j, 69);
    const spy1 = vi.spyOn(Math, 'random').mockReturnValue(0); // 9/10 档 → 63
    above.evo.convert(4, above.j, 11);
    spy1.mockRestore();
    expect(above.st.wall[above.st.idx(4, above.j)]).toBe(63);

    const below = evoAt(false);
    below.st.setWallSilent(4, below.j, 69);
    below.evo.convert(4, below.j, 11);
    expect(below.st.wall[below.st.idx(4, below.j)]).toBe(64); // 地下档 64

    const stone = evoAt(false);
    stone.st.setWallSilent(6, stone.j, 83); // 猩红石墙 ∈ Stone 集 → 349
    stone.evo.convert(6, stone.j, 11);
    expect(stone.st.wall[stone.st.idx(6, stone.j)]).toBe(349);
  });
});
```
